2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInternLM3 has open-sourced an 8-billion parameter instruction model, InternLM3-8B-Instruct, designed for general-purpose usage and advanced reasoning. This model has the following characteristics:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnhanced performance at reduced cost\u003c/strong\u003e:\nState-of-the-art performance on reasoning and knowledge-intensive tasks surpass models like Llama3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B. Remarkably, InternLM3 is trained on only 4 trillion high-quality tokens, saving more than 75% of the training cost compared to other LLMs of similar scale.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeep thinking capability\u003c/strong\u003e:\nInternLM3 supports both the deep thinking mode for solving complicated reasoning tasks via the long chain-of-thought and the normal response mode for fluent user interactions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eNews\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-news\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: News\" href=\"#news\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2025.01.15] We release InternLM3-8B-Instruct, See \u003ca href=\"#model-zoo\"\u003emodel zoo below\u003c/a\u003e for download or \u003ca href=\"/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards\"\u003emodel cards\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.08.01] We release InternLM2.5-1.8B, InternLM2.5-1.8B-Chat, InternLM2.5-20B and InternLM2.5-20B-Chat. See \u003ca href=\"#model-zoo\"\u003emodel zoo below\u003c/a\u003e for download or \u003ca href=\"/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards\"\u003emodel cards\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.07.19] We release the InternLM2-Reward series of reward models in 1.8B, 7B and 20B sizes. See \u003ca href=\"#model-zoo\"\u003emodel zoo below\u003c/a\u003e for download or \u003ca href=\"/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards/internlm2_reward.md\"\u003emodel cards\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.07.03] We release InternLM2.5-7B, InternLM2.5-7B-Chat and InternLM2.5-7B-Chat-1M. See \u003ca href=\"#model-zoo\"\u003emodel zoo below\u003c/a\u003e for download or \u003ca href=\"/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards\"\u003emodel cards\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.03.26] We release InternLM2 technical report. See \u003ca href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17297\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003earXiv\u003c/a\u003e for details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.01.31] We release InternLM2-1.8B, along with the associated chat model. They provide a cheaper deployment option while maintaining leading performance.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.01.23] We release InternLM2-Math-7B and InternLM2-Math-20B with pretraining and SFT checkpoints. They surpass ChatGPT with small sizes. See \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/InternLM/internlm-math\"\u003eInternLM-Math\u003c/a\u003e for details and download.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2024.01.17] We release InternLM2-7B and InternLM2-20B and their corresponding chat models with stronger capabilities in all dimensions. See \u003ca href=\"#model-zoo\"\u003emodel zoo below\u003c/a\u003e for download or \u003ca href=\"/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards\"\u003emodel cards\u003c/a\u003e for more details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2023.12.13] InternLM-7B-Chat and InternLM-20B-Chat checkpoints are updated. With an improved finetuning strategy, the new chat models can generate higher quality responses with greater stylistic diversity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e[2023.09.20] InternLM-20B is released with base and chat versions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eModel dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNotes:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe release of InternLM2.5 series contains 1.8B, 7B, and 20B versions. 7B models are efficient for research and application and 20B models are more powerful and can support more complex scenarios. The relation of these models are shown as follows.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInternLM2.5\u003c/strong\u003e: Foundation models pre-trained on large-scale corpus. InternLM2.5 models are recommended for consideration in most applications.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInternLM2.5-Chat\u003c/strong\u003e: The Chat model that undergoes supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), based on the InternLM2.5 model. InternLM2.5-Chat is optimized for instruction following, chat experience, and function call, which is recommended for downstream applications.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInternLM2.5-Chat-1M\u003c/strong\u003e: InternLM2.5-Chat-1M supports 1M long-context with compatible performance as InternLM2.5-Chat.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLimitations:\u003c/strong\u003e Although we have made efforts to ensure the safety of the model during the training process and to encourage the model to generate text that complies with ethical and legal requirements, the model may still produce unexpected outputs due to its size and probabilistic generation paradigm. For example, the generated responses may contain biases, discrimination, or other harmful content. Please do not propagate such content. We are not responsible for any consequences resulting from the dissemination of harmful information.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSupplements:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003eHF\u003c/code\u003e refers to the format used by HuggingFace in \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/huggingface/transformers\"\u003etransformers\u003c/a\u003e, whereas \u003ccode\u003eOrigin\u003c/code\u003e denotes the format adopted by the InternLM team in \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/InternLM/InternEvo\"\u003eInternEvo\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/details\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInternLM2-Reward\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-internlm2-reward\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: InternLM2-Reward\" href=\"#internlm2-reward\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n    \u003csummary\u003e(click to expand)\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInternLM2-Reward is a series of reward models, trained on 2.4 million preference samples, available in 1.8B, 7B, and 20B sizes. These model were applied to the PPO training process of our chat models. 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo load the InternLM3 8B Instruct model using Transformers, use the following code:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nmodel_dir = \u0026quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct\u0026quot;\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True)\n# Set `torch_dtype=torch.float16` to load model in float16, otherwise it will be loaded as float32 and might cause OOM Error.\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n# (Optional) If on low resource devices, you can load model in 4-bit or 8-bit to further save GPU memory via bitsandbytes.\n  # InternLM3 8B in 4bit will cost nearly 8GB GPU memory.\n  # pip install -U bitsandbytes\n  # 8-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_8bit=True)\n  # 4-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_4bit=True)\nmodel = model.eval()\nsystem_prompt = \u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).\n- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.\n- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\nmessages = [\n    {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;system\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: system_prompt},\n    {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai\u0026quot;},\n ]\ntokenized_chat = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, return_tensors=\u0026quot;pt\u0026quot;)\ngenerated_ids = model.generate(tokenized_chat, max_new_tokens=1024, temperature=1, repetition_penalty=1.005, top_k=40, top_p=0.8)\ngenerated_ids = [\n    output_ids[len(input_ids):] for input_ids, output_ids in zip(tokenized_chat, generated_ids)\n]\nprompt = tokenizer.batch_decode(tokenized_chat)[0]\nprint(prompt)\nresponse = tokenizer.batch_decode(generated_ids)[0]\nprint(response)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan MMDeploy teams.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"pip install lmdeploy\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003epip install lmdeploy\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYou can run batch inference locally with the following python code:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import lmdeploy\nmodel_dir = \u0026quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct\u0026quot;\npipe = lmdeploy.pipeline(model_dir)\nresponse = pipe(\u0026quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai\u0026quot;)\nprint(response)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elmdeploy\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel_dir\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003epipe\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003elmdeploy\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003epipeline\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel_dir\u003c/span\u003e)\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eresponse\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003epipe\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai\"\u003c/span\u003e)\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eprint\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eresponse\u003c/span\u003e)\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOr you can launch an OpenAI compatible server with the following command:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"lmdeploy serve api_server internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct --model-name internlm3-8b-instruct --server-port 23333\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003elmdeploy serve api_server internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct --model-name internlm3-8b-instruct --server-port 23333\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp code:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import ollama\n\nsystem_prompt = \u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).\n- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.\n- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\n\nmessages = [\n    {\n        \u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;system\u0026quot;,\n        \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: system_prompt,\n    },\n    {\n        \u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;,\n        \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai\u0026quot;\n    },\n]\n\nstream = ollama.chat(\n    model='internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct',\n    messages=messages,\n    stream=True,\n)\n\nfor chunk in stream:\n  print(chunk['message']['content'], end='', flush=True)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eollama\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003esystem_prompt\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\"\"You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.\"\"\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emessages\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e [\n    {\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"role\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"system\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"content\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003esystem_prompt\u003c/span\u003e,\n    },\n    {\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"role\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"user\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n        \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"content\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai\"\u003c/span\u003e\n    },\n]\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003estream\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eollama\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003echat\u003c/span\u003e(\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct'\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emessages\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emessages\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003estream\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n)\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efor\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003echunk\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ein\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003estream\u003c/span\u003e:\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eprint\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003echunk\u003c/span\u003e[\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'message'\u003c/span\u003e][\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'content'\u003c/span\u003e], \u003cspan \u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).\n- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"thinking_system_prompt = \u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;You are an expert mathematician with extensive experience in mathematical competitions. You approach problems through systematic thinking and rigorous reasoning. When solving problems, follow these thought processes:\n## Deep Understanding\nTake time to fully comprehend the problem before attempting a solution. Consider:\n- What is the real question being asked?\n- What are the given conditions and what do they tell us?\n- Are there any special restrictions or assumptions?\n- Which information is crucial and which is supplementary?\n## Multi-angle Analysis\nBefore solving, conduct thorough analysis:\n- What mathematical concepts and properties are involved?\n- Can you recall similar classic problems or solution methods?\n- Would diagrams or tables help visualize the problem?\n- Are there special cases that need separate consideration?\n## Systematic Thinking\nPlan your solution path:\n- Propose multiple possible approaches\n- Analyze the feasibility and merits of each method\n- Choose the most appropriate method and explain why\n- Break complex problems into smaller, manageable steps\n## Rigorous Proof\nDuring the solution process:\n- Provide solid justification for each step\n- Include detailed proofs for key conclusions\n- Pay attention to logical connections\n- Be vigilant about potential oversights\n## Repeated Verification\nAfter completing your solution:\n- Verify your results satisfy all conditions\n- Check for overlooked special cases\n- Consider if the solution can be optimized or simplified\n- Review your reasoning process\nRemember:\n1. Take time to think thoroughly rather than rushing to an answer\n2. Rigorously prove each key conclusion\n3. Keep an open mind and try different approaches\n4. Summarize valuable problem-solving methods\n5. Maintain healthy skepticism and verify multiple times\nYour response should reflect deep mathematical understanding and precise logical thinking, making your solution path and reasoning clear to others.\nWhen you're ready, present your complete solution with:\n- Clear problem understanding\n- Detailed solution process\n- Key insights\n- Thorough verification\nFocus on clear, logical progression of ideas and thorough explanation of your mathematical reasoning. Provide answers in the same language as the user asking the question, repeat the final answer using a '\\\\boxed{}' without any units, you have [[8192]] tokens to complete the answer.\n\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\u0026quot;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ethinking_system_prompt\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\"\"You are an expert mathematician with extensive experience in mathematical competitions. You approach problems through systematic thinking and rigorous reasoning. data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"import torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nmodel_dir = \u0026quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct\u0026quot;\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True)\n# Set `torch_dtype=torch.float16` to load model in float16, otherwise it will be loaded as float32 and might cause OOM Error.\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n# (Optional) If on low resource devices, you can load model in 4-bit or 8-bit to further save GPU memory via bitsandbytes.\n  # InternLM3 8B in 4bit will cost nearly 8GB GPU memory.\n  # pip install -U bitsandbytes\n  # 8-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_8bit=True)\n  # 4-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_4bit=True)\nmodel = model.eval()\nmessages = [\n    {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;system\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: thinking_system_prompt},\n    {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Given the function\\(f(x)=\\mathrm{e}^{x}-ax - a^{3}\\),\\n(1) When \\(a = 1\\), find the equation of the tangent line to the curve \\(y = f(x)\\) at the point \\((1,f(1))\\).\\n(2) If \\(f(x)\\) has a local minimum and the minimum value is less than \\(0\\), determine the range of values for \\(a\\).\u0026quot;},\n ]\ntokenized_chat = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, return_tensors=\u0026quot;pt\u0026quot;)\ngenerated_ids = model.generate(tokenized_chat, max_new_tokens=8192)\ngenerated_ids = [\n    output_ids[len(input_ids):] for input_ids, output_ids in zip(tokenized_chat, generated_ids)\n]\nprompt = tokenizer.batch_decode(tokenized_chat)[0]\nprint(prompt)\nresponse = tokenizer.batch_decode(generated_ids)[0]\nprint(response)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams\nllm = LLM(model=\u0026quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct\u0026quot;)\nsampling_params = SamplingParams(temperature=1, repetition_penalty=1.005, top_k=40, top_p=0.8, max_tokens=8192)\nprompts = [\n    {\n        \u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;system\u0026quot;,\n        \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: thinking_system_prompt,\n    },\n    {\n        \u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;,\n        \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Given the function\\(f(x)=\\mathrm{e}^{x}-ax - a^{3}\\),\\n(1) When \\(a = 1\\), find the equation of the tangent line to the curve \\(y = f(x)\\) at the point \\((1,f(1))\\).\\n(2) If \\(f(x)\\) has a local minimum and the minimum value is less than \\(0\\), determine the range of values for \\(a\\).\u0026quot;\n    },\n]\noutputs = llm.chat(prompts,\n                   sampling_params=sampling_params,\n                   use_tqdm=False)\nprint(outputs)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e1\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003erepetition_penalty\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Introduction</h2><a id="user-content-introduction" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Introduction" href="#introduction"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">InternLM3 has open-sourced an 8-billion parameter instruction model, InternLM3-8B-Instruct, designed for general-purpose usage and advanced reasoning. This model has the following characteristics:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Enhanced performance at reduced cost</strong>:
State-of-the-art performance on reasoning and knowledge-intensive tasks surpass models like Llama3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B. Remarkably, InternLM3 is trained on only 4 trillion high-quality tokens, saving more than 75% of the training cost compared to other LLMs of similar scale.</li>
<li><strong>Deep thinking capability</strong>:
InternLM3 supports both the deep thinking mode for solving complicated reasoning tasks via the long chain-of-thought and the normal response mode for fluent user interactions.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">News</h2><a id="user-content-news" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: News" href="#news"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">[2025.01.15] We release InternLM3-8B-Instruct, See <a href="#model-zoo">model zoo below</a> for download or <a href="/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards">model cards</a> for more details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.08.01] We release InternLM2.5-1.8B, InternLM2.5-1.8B-Chat, InternLM2.5-20B and InternLM2.5-20B-Chat. See <a href="#model-zoo">model zoo below</a> for download or <a href="/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards">model cards</a> for more details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.07.19] We release the InternLM2-Reward series of reward models in 1.8B, 7B and 20B sizes. See <a href="#model-zoo">model zoo below</a> for download or <a href="/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards/internlm2_reward.md">model cards</a> for more details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.07.03] We release InternLM2.5-7B, InternLM2.5-7B-Chat and InternLM2.5-7B-Chat-1M. See <a href="#model-zoo">model zoo below</a> for download or <a href="/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards">model cards</a> for more details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.03.26] We release InternLM2 technical report. See <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17297" rel="nofollow">arXiv</a> for details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.01.31] We release InternLM2-1.8B, along with the associated chat model. They provide a cheaper deployment option while maintaining leading performance.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.01.23] We release InternLM2-Math-7B and InternLM2-Math-20B with pretraining and SFT checkpoints. They surpass ChatGPT with small sizes. See <a href="https://github.com/InternLM/internlm-math">InternLM-Math</a> for details and download.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2024.01.17] We release InternLM2-7B and InternLM2-20B and their corresponding chat models with stronger capabilities in all dimensions. See <a href="#model-zoo">model zoo below</a> for download or <a href="/InternLM/InternLM/blob/main/model_cards">model cards</a> for more details.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2023.12.13] InternLM-7B-Chat and InternLM-20B-Chat checkpoints are updated. With an improved finetuning strategy, the new chat models can generate higher quality responses with greater stylistic diversity.</p>
<p dir="auto">[2023.09.20] InternLM-20B is released with base and chat versions.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Model Zoo</h2><a id="user-content-model-zoo" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Model Zoo" href="#model-zoo"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">InternLM3</h3><a id="user-content-internlm3" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: InternLM3" href="#internlm3"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Notes:</strong></p>
<p dir="auto">The release of InternLM2.5 series contains 1.8B, 7B, and 20B versions. 7B models are efficient for research and application and 20B models are more powerful and can support more complex scenarios. The relation of these models are shown as follows.</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>InternLM2.5</strong>: Foundation models pre-trained on large-scale corpus. InternLM2.5 models are recommended for consideration in most applications.</li>
<li><strong>InternLM2.5-Chat</strong>: The Chat model that undergoes supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), based on the InternLM2.5 model. InternLM2.5-Chat is optimized for instruction following, chat experience, and function call, which is recommended for downstream applications.</li>
<li><strong>InternLM2.5-Chat-1M</strong>: InternLM2.5-Chat-1M supports 1M long-context with compatible performance as InternLM2.5-Chat.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Limitations:</strong> Although we have made efforts to ensure the safety of the model during the training process and to encourage the model to generate text that complies with ethical and legal requirements, the model may still produce unexpected outputs due to its size and probabilistic generation paradigm. For example, the generated responses may contain biases, discrimination, or other harmful content. Please do not propagate such content. We are not responsible for any consequences resulting from the dissemination of harmful information.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Supplements:</strong> <code>HF</code> refers to the format used by HuggingFace in <a href="https://github.com/huggingface/transformers">transformers</a>, whereas <code>Origin</code> denotes the format adopted by the InternLM team in <a href="https://github.com/InternLM/InternEvo">InternEvo</a>.</p>
</details>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Performance</h2><a id="user-content-performance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Performance" href="#performance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We conducted a comprehensive evaluation of InternLM using the open-source evaluation tool <a href="https://github.com/internLM/OpenCompass/">OpenCompass</a>. The evaluation covered five dimensions of capabilities: disciplinary competence, language competence, knowledge competence, inference competence, and comprehension competence. Here are some of the evaluation results, and you can visit the <a href="https://rank.opencompass.org.cn" rel="nofollow">OpenCompass leaderboard</a> for more evaluation results.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h3><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Transformers inference</h4><a id="user-content-transformers-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Transformers inference" href="#transformers-inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To load the InternLM3 8B Instruct model using Transformers, use the following code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM
model_dir = &quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct&quot;
tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True)
# Set `torch_dtype=torch.float16` to load model in float16, otherwise it will be loaded as float32 and might cause OOM Error.
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, trust_remote_code=True, torch_dtype=torch.float16)
# (Optional) If on low resource devices, you can load model in 4-bit or 8-bit to further save GPU memory via bitsandbytes.
  # InternLM3 8B in 4bit will cost nearly 8GB GPU memory.
  # pip install -U bitsandbytes
  # 8-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=&quot;auto&quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_8bit=True)
  # 4-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map=&quot;auto&quot;, trust_remote_code=True, load_in_4bit=True)
model = model.eval()
system_prompt = &quot;&quot;&quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).
- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.
- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.&quot;&quot;&quot;
messages = [
    {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: system_prompt},
    {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai&quot;},
 ]
tokenized_chat = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=True, add_generation_prompt=True, return_tensors=&quot;pt&quot;)
generated_ids = model.generate(tokenized_chat, max_new_tokens=1024, temperature=1, repetition_penalty=1.005, top_k=40, top_p=0.8)
generated_ids = [
    output_ids[len(input_ids):] for input_ids, output_ids in zip(tokenized_chat, generated_ids)
]
prompt = tokenizer.batch_decode(tokenized_chat)[0]
print(prompt)
response = tokenizer.batch_decode(generated_ids)[0]
print(response)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">torch</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoTokenizer</span>, <span class="pl-v">AutoModelForCausalLM</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model_dir</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoTokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_pretrained</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model_dir</span>, <span class="pl-s1">trust_remote_code</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># Set `torch_dtype=torch.float16` to load model in float16, otherwise it will be loaded as float32 and might cause OOM Error.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoModelForCausalLM</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_pretrained</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model_dir</span>, <span class="pl-s1">trust_remote_code</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">torch_dtype</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">torch</span>.<span class="pl-c1">float16</span>)
<span class="pl-c"># (Optional) If on low resource devices, you can load model in 4-bit or 8-bit to further save GPU memory via bitsandbytes.</span>
  <span class="pl-c"># InternLM3 8B in 4bit will cost nearly 8GB GPU memory.</span>
  <span class="pl-c"># pip install -U bitsandbytes</span>
  <span class="pl-c"># 8-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map="auto", trust_remote_code=True, load_in_8bit=True)</span>
  <span class="pl-c"># 4-bit: model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_dir, device_map="auto", trust_remote_code=True, load_in_4bit=True)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">eval</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文."""</span>
<span class="pl-s1">messages</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [
    {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"system"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span>},
    {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai"</span>},
 ]
<span class="pl-s1">tokenized_chat</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">apply_chat_template</span>(<span class="pl-s1">messages</span>, <span class="pl-s1">tokenize</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">add_generation_prompt</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">return_tensors</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"pt"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">generate</span>(<span class="pl-s1">tokenized_chat</span>, <span class="pl-s1">max_new_tokens</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1024</span>, <span class="pl-s1">temperature</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">repetition_penalty</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1.005</span>, <span class="pl-s1">top_k</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">40</span>, <span class="pl-s1">top_p</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.8</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [
    <span class="pl-s1">output_ids</span>[<span class="pl-en">len</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input_ids</span>):] <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">input_ids</span>, <span class="pl-s1">output_ids</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-en">zip</span>(<span class="pl-s1">tokenized_chat</span>, <span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span>)
]
<span class="pl-s1">prompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">batch_decode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">tokenized_chat</span>)[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>]
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">prompt</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">batch_decode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span>)[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>]
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">LMDeploy inference</h4><a id="user-content-lmdeploy-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: LMDeploy inference" href="#lmdeploy-inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">LMDeploy is a toolkit for compressing, deploying, and serving LLM, developed by the MMRazor and MMDeploy teams.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install lmdeploy"><pre>pip install lmdeploy</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You can run batch inference locally with the following python code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import lmdeploy
model_dir = &quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct&quot;
pipe = lmdeploy.pipeline(model_dir)
response = pipe(&quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai&quot;)
print(response)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">lmdeploy</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model_dir</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">pipe</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">lmdeploy</span>.<span class="pl-c1">pipeline</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model_dir</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">pipe</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai"</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or you can launch an OpenAI compatible server with the following command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="lmdeploy serve api_server internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct --model-name internlm3-8b-instruct --server-port 23333"><pre>lmdeploy serve api_server internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct --model-name internlm3-8b-instruct --server-port 23333</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then you can send a chat request to the server:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl http://localhost:23333/v1/chat/completions \
    -H &quot;Content-Type: application/json&quot; \
    -d '{
    &quot;model&quot;: &quot;internlm3-8b-instruct&quot;,
    &quot;messages&quot;: [
    {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai&quot;}
    ]
    }'"><pre>curl http://localhost:23333/v1/chat/completions \
    -H <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Content-Type: application/json<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> \
    -d <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{</span>
<span class="pl-s">    "model": "internlm3-8b-instruct",</span>
<span class="pl-s">    "messages": [</span>
<span class="pl-s">    {"role": "user", "content": "Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai"}</span>
<span class="pl-s">    ]</span>
<span class="pl-s">    }<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Find more details in the <a href="https://lmdeploy.readthedocs.io/en/latest/" rel="nofollow">LMDeploy documentation</a></p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h5 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OpenAI client</h5><a id="user-content-openai-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenAI client" href="#openai-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import openai
client = openai.Client(
    base_url=&quot;http://127.0.0.1:30000/v1&quot;, api_key=&quot;EMPTY&quot;)

# Chat completion
response = client.chat.completions.create(
    model=&quot;default&quot;,
    messages=[
        {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;You are a helpful AI assistant&quot;},
        {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;List 3 countries and their capitals.&quot;},
    ],
    temperature=0,
    max_tokens=64,
)
print(response)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Client</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">base_url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"http://127.0.0.1:30000/v1"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"EMPTY"</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># Chat completion</span>
<span class="pl-s1">response</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"default"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"system"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"You are a helpful AI assistant"</span>},
        {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"List 3 countries and their capitals."</span>},
    ],
    <span class="pl-s1">temperature</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">max_tokens</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">64</span>,
)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ollama inference</h4><a id="user-content-ollama-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ollama inference" href="#ollama-inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">install ollama and pull the model</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# install ollama
curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh
# pull the model
ollama pull internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct
# install ollama-python
pip install ollama"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install ollama</span>
curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> pull the model</span>
ollama pull internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install ollama-python</span>
pip install ollama</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">inference code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import ollama

system_prompt = &quot;&quot;&quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).
- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.
- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.&quot;&quot;&quot;

messages = [
    {
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: system_prompt,
    },
    {
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai&quot;
    },
]

stream = ollama.chat(
    model='internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct',
    messages=messages,
    stream=True,
)

for chunk in stream:
  print(chunk['message']['content'], end='', flush=True)"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">ollama</span>

<span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文."""</span>

<span class="pl-s1">messages</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [
    {
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"system"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span>,
    },
    {
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai"</span>
    },
]

<span class="pl-s1">stream</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ollama</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">'internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct'</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">messages</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">stream</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
)

<span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">chunk</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-s1">stream</span>:
  <span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">chunk</span>[<span class="pl-s">'message'</span>][<span class="pl-s">'content'</span>], <span class="pl-s1">end</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">''</span>, <span class="pl-s1">flush</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">vLLM inference</h4><a id="user-content-vllm-inference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: vLLM inference" href="#vllm-inference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">refer to <a href="https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/getting_started/installation/index.html" rel="nofollow">installation</a> to install the latest code of vllm</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install vllm --pre --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/nightly"><pre><span class="pl-s1">pip</span> <span class="pl-s1">install</span> <span class="pl-s1">vllm</span> <span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">pre</span> <span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">extra</span><span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">index</span><span class="pl-c1">-</span><span class="pl-s1">url</span> <span class="pl-s1">https</span>:<span class="pl-c1">//</span><span class="pl-s1">wheels</span>.<span class="pl-c1">vllm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">ai</span><span class="pl-c1">/</span><span class="pl-s1">nightly</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">inference code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
llm = LLM(model=&quot;internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct&quot;)
sampling_params = SamplingParams(temperature=1, repetition_penalty=1.005, top_k=40, top_p=0.8)
system_prompt = &quot;&quot;&quot;You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).
- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.
- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文.&quot;&quot;&quot;
prompts = [
    {
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: system_prompt,
    },
    {
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai&quot;
    },
]
outputs = llm.chat(prompts,
                   sampling_params=sampling_params,
                   use_tqdm=False)
print(outputs)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">vllm</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-c1">LLM</span>, <span class="pl-v">SamplingParams</span>
<span class="pl-s1">llm</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">LLM</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"internlm/internlm3-8b-instruct"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">sampling_params</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">SamplingParams</span>(<span class="pl-s1">temperature</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">repetition_penalty</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1.005</span>, <span class="pl-s1">top_k</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">40</span>, <span class="pl-s1">top_p</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">0.8</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""You are an AI assistant whose name is InternLM (书生·浦语).</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) is a conversational language model that is developed by Shanghai AI Laboratory (上海人工智能实验室). It is designed to be helpful, honest, and harmless.</span>
<span class="pl-s">- InternLM (书生·浦语) can understand and communicate fluently in the language chosen by the user such as English and 中文."""</span>
<span class="pl-s1">prompts</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [
    {
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"system"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">system_prompt</span>,
    },
    {
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Please tell me five scenic spots in Shanghai"</span>
    },
]
<span class="pl-s1">outputs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">llm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">prompts</span>,
                   <span class="pl-s1">sampling_params</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">sampling_params</span>,
                   <span class="pl-s1">use_tqdm</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>)
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">outputs</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Thinking Mode</h3><a id="user-content-thinking-mode" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Thinking Mode" href="#thinking-mode"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Thinking system prompt</h4><a id="user-content-thinking-system-prompt" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Thinking system prompt" href="#thinking-system-prompt"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="thinking_system_prompt = &quot;&quot;&quot;You are an expert mathematician with extensive experience in mathematical competitions. You approach problems through systematic thinking and rigorous reasoning. When solving problems, follow these thought processes:
## Deep Understanding
Take time to fully comprehend the problem before attempting a solution. Consider:
- What is the real question being asked?
- What are the given conditions and what do they tell us?
- Are there any special restrictions or assumptions?
- Which information is crucial and which is supplementary?
## Multi-angle Analysis
Before solving, conduct thorough analysis:
- What mathematical concepts and properties are involved?
- Can you recall similar classic problems or solution methods?
- Would diagrams or tables help visualize the problem?
- Are there special cases that need separate consideration?
## Systematic Thinking
Plan your solution path:
- Propose multiple possible approaches
- Analyze the feasibility and merits of each method
- Choose the most appropriate method and explain why
- Break complex problems into smaller, manageable steps
## Rigorous Proof
During the solution process:
- Provide solid justification for each step
- Include detailed proofs for key conclusions
- Pay attention to logical connections
- Be vigilant about potential oversights
## Repeated Verification
After completing your solution:
- Verify your results satisfy all conditions
- Check for overlooked special cases
- Consider if the solution can be optimized or simplified
- Review your reasoning process
Remember:
1. Take time to think thoroughly rather than rushing to an answer
2. Rigorously prove each key conclusion
3. Keep an open mind and try different approaches
4. Summarize valuable problem-solving methods
5. Maintain healthy skepticism and verify multiple times
Your response should reflect deep mathematical understanding and precise logical thinking, making your solution path and reasoning clear to others.
When you're ready, present your complete solution with:
- Clear problem understanding
- Detailed solution process
- Key insights
- Thorough verification
Focus on clear, logical progression of ideas and thorough explanation of your mathematical reasoning. Provide answers in the same language as the user asking the question, repeat the final answer using a '\\boxed{}' without any units, you have [[8192]] tokens to complete the answer.
&quot;&quot;&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-s1">thinking_system_prompt</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"""You are an expert mathematician with extensive experience in mathematical competitions. You approach problems through systematic thinking and rigorous reasoning. When solving problems, follow these thought processes:</span>
<span class="pl-s">## Deep Understanding</span>
<span class="pl-s">Take time to fully comprehend the problem before attempting a solution. Consider:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- What is the real question being asked?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- What are the given conditions and what do they tell us?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Are there any special restrictions or assumptions?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Which information is crucial and which is supplementary?</span>
<span class="pl-s">## Multi-angle Analysis</span>
<span class="pl-s">Before solving, conduct thorough analysis:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- What mathematical concepts and properties are involved?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Can you recall similar classic problems or solution methods?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Would diagrams or tables help visualize the problem?</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Are there special cases that need separate consideration?</span>
<span class="pl-s">## Systematic Thinking</span>
<span class="pl-s">Plan your solution path:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Propose multiple possible approaches</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Analyze the feasibility and merits of each method</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Choose the most appropriate method and explain why</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Break complex problems into smaller, manageable steps</span>
<span class="pl-s">## Rigorous Proof</span>
<span class="pl-s">During the solution process:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Provide solid justification for each step</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Include detailed proofs for key conclusions</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Pay attention to logical connections</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Be vigilant about potential oversights</span>
<span class="pl-s">## Repeated Verification</span>
<span class="pl-s">After completing your solution:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Verify your results satisfy all conditions</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Check for overlooked special cases</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Consider if the solution can be optimized or simplified</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Review your reasoning process</span>
<span class="pl-s">Remember:</span>
<span class="pl-s">1. Take time to think thoroughly rather than rushing to an answer</span>
<span class="pl-s">2. Rigorously prove each key conclusion</span>
<span class="pl-s">3. Keep an open mind and try different approaches</span>
<span class="pl-s">4. Summarize valuable problem-solving methods</span>
<span class="pl-s">5. Maintain healthy skepticism and verify multiple times</span>
<span class="pl-s">Your response should reflect deep mathematical understanding and precise logical thinking, making your solution path and reasoning clear to others.</span>
<span class="pl-s">When you're ready, present your complete solution with:</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Clear problem understanding</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Detailed solution process</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Key insights</span>
<span class="pl-s">- Thorough verification</span>
<span class="pl-s">Focus on clear, logical progression of ideas and thorough explanation of your mathematical reasoning. Provide answers in the same language as the user asking the question, repeat the final answer using a '<span class="pl-cce">\\</span>boxed{}' without any units, you have [[8192]] tokens to complete the answer.</span>
<span class="pl-s">"""</span></pre></div>
